        <div class="cnt-head"><h1>About Vice City Web Edition</h1></div>
        <div class="cnt-filler">
            <div class="cnt-info">
                <p>Grand Theft Auto: Vice City Web Edition brings the legendary open world experience of 1986 Miami directly into your browser. Set in a sprawling, neon-soaked metropolis inspired by 1980s Miami, Vice City stands as one of the most iconic environments in gaming history.</p> 
                
                <p>The story follows Tommy Vercetti, a mobster recently released from prison after serving fifteen years. He is sent to Vice City by his former boss Sonny Forelli to oversee a drug deal. When the deal is ambushed and both the money and the drugs are lost, Tommy is left to pick up the pieces.</p>

<p>As he works to recover what was lost and answer to the Forelli family, Tommy becomes involved with the city’s various criminal organizations and power players. Over time, he builds his own network of businesses and influence, ultimately rising to control Vice City’s criminal underworld.</p>

                <p>This technical port is built on the revcDOS codebase, which utilizes the reverse-engineered reVC engine. This version requires no downloads and no installation because assets stream dynamically as you play. It is a complete, self-contained experience that preserves every mission from the original 2002 release.</p>

                <p><strong>Key Features:</strong></p>
                <ul style="margin-bottom: 20px;">
                    <li><strong>Integrated Save Manager:</strong> You can upload your existing .b save files from a PC or PS2 to resume your progress. You can also download backups to keep your virtual progress safe.</li>
                    <li><strong>Instant Access:</strong> There are no large initial downloads. The WebAssembly engine loads in seconds and streams the map as you drive.</li>
                    <li><strong>Cross-Platform Support:</strong> Play with a standard keyboard and mouse on desktop or use integrated touch controls on mobile devices.</li>
                </ul>

    <div class="cnt-box">
        <div class="cnt-head"><h1>Frequently Asked Questions</h1></div>
        <div class="cnt-filler">
            <div class="cnt-info">
                <div class="faq-item">
                    <button class="faq-question">Is this the full game? Do I need to upload files?</button>
                    <div class="faq-answer"><p>Yes, this is the complete version of Grand Theft Auto: Vice City. It includes all missions, side activities, and the full map. You do not need to upload any game files or verify ownership. Just click play and the game assets will stream automatically to your browser.</p></div>
                </div>
                <div class="faq-item">
                    <button class="faq-question">How does the Save Manager work?</button>
                    <div class="faq-answer"><p>The built-in Save Manager allows you to control your game data. You can download your save file (GTAVCsf*.b) to your computer for safekeeping or upload an existing save file to jump instantly to a specific point in the story. This makes it easy to move progress between different devices.</p></div>
                </div>
                <div class="faq-item">
                    <button class="faq-question">Does it support controllers and mobile?</button>
                    <div class="faq-answer"><p>Yes. The game supports standard Gamepad inputs for Xbox and PlayStation controllers via USB or Bluetooth. Mobile users can play using on-screen touch controls. We recommend using landscape mode and enabling fullscreen for the best mobile experience.</p></div>
                </div>
                <div class="faq-item">
                    <button class="faq-question">Why does the game show a black screen or run poorly?</button>
                    <div class="faq-answer"><p>If the game does not start, shows a black screen, or suffers from heavy lag, make sure that <strong>Hardware Acceleration</strong> is enabled in your browser settings. This allows the game to use your graphics card (GPU) to render the 3D world. Without this setting enabled, the WebAssembly engine may fail to initialize.</p></div>
                </div>
                <div class="faq-item">
                    <button class="faq-question">Why are the physics acting weirdly or missions failing?</button>
                    <div class="faq-answer"><p>By default, the frame rate is set to 0 (unlocked) for maximum performance on modern hardware. However, the original game engine was designed to run at 30 FPS. Unlocked framerates can cause strange physics bugs, making specific missions becoming impossible to complete. If you get stuck due to physics, change the FPS limit to 30 in the game menu.</p></div>
                </div>
                <div class="faq-item">
                    <button class="faq-question">My device is getting very hot. How do I fix this?</button>
                    <div class="faq-answer"><p>Running a full 3D game in a browser can be demanding. If you are playing on a mobile device or a low-end laptop that is overheating or draining battery too quickly, we recommend restricting the frame rate to 30 or 60. This reduces the load on your processor and provides a much more stable experience for long play sessions.</p></div>
                </div>
            </div>

(function() {
    const allApps = [
        { name: "Reborn XP", url: "/apps/reborn-xp/" },
        { name: "Vice City", url: "/apps/vice-city/" },
        { name: "Minecraft", url: "/apps/minecraft/" },
        { name: "3D Pinball", url: "/apps/3d-pinball-space-cadet/" },
        { name: "Console Emulator", url: "/apps/emulator/" },
        { name: "SWF Player", url: "/apps/swf-player/" },
        { name: "Macintosh Classic", url: "/apps/minivmac/" },
        { name: "Hacker Simulator", url: "/apps/hacker-simulator/" },
        { name: "Angry Birds Chrome", url: "/apps/angry-birds-chrome/" }
    ];

    const currentPath = window.location.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, "");

    const availableApps = allApps.filter(app => {
        const appPath = app.url.replace(/\/+$/, "");
        return !currentPath.includes(appPath);
    });

    for (let i = availableApps.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
        [availableApps[i], availableApps[j]] = [availableApps[j], availableApps[i]];
    }

    const selectedApps = availableApps.slice(0, 5);
    const listContainer = document.getElementById('dynamic-apps-list');
    
    if(listContainer) {
        let html = '';
        selectedApps.forEach(app => {
            html += `<li><a href="${app.url}">${app.name}</a></li>`;
        });
        listContainer.innerHTML = html;
    }
})();
</script>

<script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
        const fullscreenBtn = document.getElementById('fullscreen-btn');
        const mobileFullscreenBtn = document.getElementById('mobile-fullscreen-btn');
        const shareBtn = document.getElementById('share-btn');
        const iframe = document.getElementById('full-iframe');
        
        const focusGame = () => {
            if(iframe && iframe.contentWindow) {
                try { 
                    iframe.contentWindow.focus(); 
                } catch(e) {
                    console.log("Focus attempt failed", e);
                }
            }
        };

        document.getElementById('full-container').addEventListener('click', focusGame);

        const toggleFullScreen = (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            
            const warning = document.getElementById('mobile-warning');
            const isFullscreen = document.fullscreenElement || document.webkitFullscreenElement;

            if (!isFullscreen) {
                iframe.style.display = 'block';
                
                if(warning) warning.style.display = 'none';

                const requestPromise = iframe.requestFullscreen 
                    ? iframe.requestFullscreen() 
                    : iframe.webkitRequestFullscreen 
                        ? iframe.webkitRequestFullscreen() 
                        : null;

                if (requestPromise) {
                    requestPromise.then(() => {
                        setTimeout(focusGame, 100);

                        if ('keyboard' in navigator && 'lock' in navigator.keyboard) {
                            navigator.keyboard.lock(['Escape']).catch(err => {
                                console.warn("Keyboard lock failed:", err);
                            });
                        }

                    }).catch(err => {
                        console.error("Fullscreen failed:", err);
                        iframe.style.display = ''; 
                        if(warning) warning.style.display = '';
                    });
                }
            } else {
                if (document.exitFullscreen) {
                    document.exitFullscreen();
                } else if (document.webkitExitFullscreen) {
                    document.webkitExitFullscreen();
                }
            }
        };

        const onFullScreenChange = () => {
            const warning = document.getElementById('mobile-warning');
            const isFullscreen = document.fullscreenElement || document.webkitFullscreenElement;

            if (!isFullscreen) {
                iframe.style.display = ''; 
                warning.style.display = '';

                if ('keyboard' in navigator && 'unlock' in navigator.keyboard) {
                    navigator.keyboard.unlock();
                }

            } else {
                setTimeout(focusGame, 200);
            }
        };

        document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', onFullScreenChange);
        document.addEventListener('webkitfullscreenchange', onFullScreenChange);
        document.addEventListener('mozfullscreenchange', onFullScreenChange);

        if (fullscreenBtn) fullscreenBtn.addEventListener('click', toggleFullScreen);
        if (mobileFullscreenBtn) mobileFullscreenBtn.addEventListener('click', toggleFullScreen);

        if (shareBtn) {
            shareBtn.addEventListener('click', async e => {
                e.preventDefault();
                const shareData = { title: 'Vice City Web - GTA Vice City in Browser', text: 'Full GTA Vice City running in your browser with save manager!', url: window.location.href };
                try {
                    if (navigator.share) { await navigator.share(shareData); } else {
                        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.href);
                        alert('Link copied to clipboard!');
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error('Share error:', err);
                    navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.href);
                    alert('Could not share. Link copied to clipboard instead!');
                }
            });
        }
    });

    const faqItems = document.querySelectorAll('.faq-item');
    faqItems.forEach(item => {
        const question = item.querySelector('.faq-question');
        const answer = item.querySelector('.faq-answer');
        question.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const is_open = item.classList.toggle('open');
            answer.style.maxHeight = is_open ? answer.scrollHeight + "px" : null;
        });
    });
</script>

    const loader = document.getElementById('global-loader');
    if (loader) {
      setTimeout(function() {
        loader.classList.add('loader-hidden');
      }, 500);
    }
  });
</script>
